  59478 bootloadervalidate
  59490 Validate the bootloader image
  594b0 [image_address]
  594c0 Validates the bootloader image.  Image must have bootloader header.
  59504 Validates header and image crc32
  59528 bootloaderupdate
  59540 Update the bootloader in flash
  59560 [nand | spi] [image_address] [image_length]
  5958c Updates the the bootloader in flash.  Uses bootloader header if present
  595d4 to validate image.
  595e7 where:
  595ee   nand          - forces updating the NAND bootloader (optional)
  5962f   spi           - forces updating the SPI bootloader (optional)
  5966f   image_address - address image is located in RAM
  596a1   image_size    - size of image in hex
  596c9 If the image size and address are not specified then
  596fe $(fileaddr) and $(filesize) will be used.

  5995d     - Allocate a named bootmem block with a given name and size at an
  599a3       optional fixed address.  Sets environment variables named_block_addr,
  599ef       named_block_size to address and size of block allocated.

 1ccb90 Boot from an Octeon Executive ELF image in memory
 1ccbc8  [elf_address [stack=stack_size] [heap=heap_size] [coremask=mask_to_run | numcores=core_cnt_to_run] [forceboot] [debug] [break] [endbootargs] [app name] [app_args ...] .
 1ccc72 elf_address - address of ELF image to load. defaults to $(loadaddr). If 0, default load address used.
 1cccd8 stack       - size of stack in bytes.  Defaults to 1 megabyte
 1ccd16 heap        - size of heap in bytes.  Defaults to 3 megabytes
 1ccd54 coremask    - mask of cores to run on.  Anded with coremask_override environment
 1ccda5               variable to ensure only working cores are used
 1ccde2 numcores    - number of cores to run on.  Runs on specified number of cores, taking into
 1cce3b               account the coremask_override.
 1cce68 skipcores   - only meaningful with numcores.  Skips this many cores (starting from 0) when
 1ccec3               loading the numcores cores. For example, setting skipcores to 1 will skip core 0
 1ccf22               and load the application starting at the next available core.
 1ccf6e debug       - if present, bootloader passes debug flag to application which will cause
 1ccfc5               the application to stop at a breakpoint on startup
 1cd006 break       - if present, exit program when control-c is received on the console
 1cd057 forceboot   - if set, boots application even if core 0 is not in mask
 1cd09d endbootargs - if set, bootloader does not process any further arguments and only passes
 1cd0f5               the arguments that follow to the application.  If not set, the application
 1cd14e               receives all command line as arguments.
 1cd184 app name    - U-boot has no way of knowing the name of the application so this must be the first argument.

 1ce410 bootoctelf
 1ce420 Boot a generic ELF image in memory. NOTE: This command does not
 1ce460               support simple executive applications, use bootoct for those.
 1ce4b0 elf_address [mem_size [coremask=mask_to_run | numcores=core_cnt_to_run] [skipcores=core_cnt_to_skip] [forceboot] ]
 1ce523 elf_address - address of ELF image to load.  If 0, default load address is
 1ce56e               used.
 1ce582 mem_size    - amount of memory to reserve for ELF file.  Starts at ELF
 1ce5c9               entry point rounded down to 1 MByte alignment.
 1ce606               No memory allocated if not specified.  (Program will not
 1ce64d               coexist with simple executive applications or Linux if memory
 1ce699               is not allocated properly.
 1ce6c2 coremask    - mask of cores to run on.  Anded with coremask_override
 1ce707               environment variable to ensure only working cores are used.
 1ce751               Note: Most ELF files are only suitable for booting on a
 1ce797               single core.
 1ce7b2 forceboot   - if set, boots application even if core 0 is not in mask
 1ce7f8 numcores    - number of cores to run on.  Runs on specified number of
 1ce83e               cores, taking into account the coremask_override.
 1ce87e skipcores   - only meaningful with numcores.  Skips this many cores
 1ce8c2               (starting from 0) when loading the numcores cores.
 1ce903               For example, setting skipcores to 1 will skip core 0
 1ce946               and load the application starting at the next available core.

 1cf7c8 load binary file from a dos filesystem
 1cf7f0 <interface> [<dev[:part]>]  <addr> <filename> [bytes [pos]]
 1cf82c     - Load binary file 'filename' from 'dev' on 'interface'
 1cf868       to address 'addr' from dos filesystem.
 1cf895       'pos' gives the file position to start loading from.
 1cf8d0       If 'pos' is omitted, 0 is used. 'pos' requires 'bytes'.
 1cf90e       'bytes' gives the size to load. If 'bytes' is 0 or omitted,
 1cf950       the load stops on end of file.

 1cf9a9       ARCH_DMA_MINALIGN then a misaligned buffer warning will
 1cf9e7       be printed and performance will suffer for the load.
 1cfa22       All numeric parameters are assumed to be hex.
